USDC returns a bool on\n///         transfer/transferFrom, so we check it explicitly.\ninterface IERC20 {\n    function transfer(address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool);\n    function transferFrom(address from, address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool);\n    function balanceOf(address account) external view returns (uint256);\n    function allowance(address owner, address spender) external view returns (uint256);\n    function decimals() external view returns (uint8);\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD4kWiAi48q4muGbxkvA","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01D9pJHN1gPnBHwsu6nVij4j","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-20T003606Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/contracts/src/TipJar.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.24;\n\nimport {IERC20} from \"./IERC20.sol\";\n\n/// @title TipJar\n/// @notice Accepts USDC tips and keeps an onchain feed of them. The USDC token\n///         address is set at construction, so the same contract works against\n///         real Base USDC (0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913) or a\n///         local mock token for development.\ncontract TipJar {\n    struct Tip {\n        address sender;\n        uint256 amount;\n        uint256 timestamp;\n        string message;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice The USDC token this jar accepts.\n    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n\n    /// @notice Owner may withdraw collected tips.\n    address public owner;\n\n    /// @notice The full tip feed, oldest first.\n    Tip[] private _tips;\n\n    /// @notice Running total of all tips ever received (in USDC's smallest unit).\n    uint256 public totalTipped;\n\n    /// @notice Total tipped per address.\n    mapping(address => uint256) public tippedBy;\n\n    event NewTip(\n        uint256 indexed index,\n        address indexed sender,\n        uint256 amount,\n        string message,\n        uint256 timestamp\n    );\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n    event OwnershipTransferred(address indexed previousOwner, address indexed newOwner);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error TransferFailed();\n    error NotOwner();\n    error ZeroAddress();\n\n    modifier onlyOwner() {\n        if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();\n        _;\n    }\n\n    constructor(address usdcAddress) {\n        if (usdcAddress == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        usdc = IERC20(usdcAddress);\n        owner = msg.sender;\n        emit OwnershipTransferred(address(0), msg.sender);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Send a USDC tip with an optional message.\n    /// @dev Caller must `approve` this contract for `amount` on the USDC token first.\n    /// @param amount USDC amount in the token's smallest unit (USDC has 6 decimals).\n    /// @param message A short public message attached to the tip.\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        // USDC returns true on success; revert on any non-true result.\n        bool ok = usdc.transferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n        if (!ok) revert TransferFailed();\n\n        uint256 index = _tips.length;\n        _tips.push(\n            Tip({\n                sender: msg.sender,\n                amount: amount,\n                timestamp: block.timestamp,\n                message: message\n            })\n        );\n        totalTipped += amount;\n        tippedBy[msg.sender] += amount;\n\n        emit NewTip(index, msg.sender, amount, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Number of tips in the feed.\n    function tipsCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _tips.length;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Read a single tip by index.\n    function getTip(uint256 index) external view returns (Tip memory) {\n        return _tips[index];\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Return up to `limit` most recent tips, newest first.\n    /// @param limit Maximum number of tips to return.\n    function getRecentTips(uint256 limit) external view returns (Tip[] memory) {\n        uint256 total = _tips.length;\n        uint256 n = limit < total ? limit : total;\n        Tip[] memory out = new Tip[](n);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n            out[i] = _tips[total - 1 - i];\n        }\n        return out;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Current USDC balance held by the jar.\n    function balance() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return usdc.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Owner withdraws `amount` of collected USDC to their address.\n    function withdraw(uint256 amount) external onlyOwner {\n        bool ok = usdc.transfer(owner, amount);\n        if (!ok) revert TransferFailed();\n        emit Withdrawn(owner, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Transfer ownership to a new address.\n    function transferOwnership(address newOwner) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newOwner == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        emit OwnershipTransferred(owner, newOwner);\n        owner = newOwner;\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":498,"cache_read_input_tokens":19243,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":498},"output_tokens":20,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1c398c09-4d5b-4f28-a0e9-8dda39bfa970","uuid":"2e00fc53-5157-48fd-aeb7-16bb8c66cc55","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:37:34.475Z","request_id":"req_011CeD4kVw3PuiPfUjwUhMsu"}

i++) {\n            out[i] = _tips[total - 1 - i];\n        }\n        return out;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Current USDC balance held by the jar.\n    function balance() external view returns onlyOwner {\n        bool ok = usdc.transfer(owner, amount);\n        if (!ok) revert TransferFailed();\n        emit Withdrawn(owner, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Transfer ownership to a new address.\n    function transferOwnership(address newOwner) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newOwner == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        emit OwnershipTransferred(owner, newOwner);\n        owner = newOwner;\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.24;\n\n/// @title MockUSDC\n/// @notice A tiny 6-decimal ERC20 that mimics USDC for LOCAL development only.\n///         This is never meant to be deployed to a real network — on Base you\n///         point the TipJar at real USDC (0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913).\ncontract MockUSDC {\n    string public constant name = \"Mock USD Coin\";\n    string public constant symbol = \"USDC\";\n    uint8 public constant decimals = 6;\n\n    uint256 public totalSupply;\n    mapping(address => uint256) public balanceOf;\n    mapping(address => mapping(address => uint256)) public allowance;\n\n    event Transfer(address indexed from, address indexed to, uint256 value);\n    event Approval(address indexed owner, address indexed spender, uint256 value);\n\n    /// @notice Anyone can mint mock USDC to themselves for testing.\n    function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external {\n        totalSupply += amount;\n        balanceOf[to] += amount;\n        emit Transfer(address(0), to, amount);\n    }\n\n    function approve(address spender, uint256 amount) external returns (bool) {\n        allowance[msg.sender][spender] = amount;\n        emit Approval(msg.sender, spender, amount);\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    function transfer(address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool) {\n        _transfer(msg.sender, to, amount);\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    function transferFrom(address from, address to, uint256 amount) external returns (bool) {\n        uint256 allowed = allowance[from][msg.sender];\n        require(allowed >= amount, \"insufficient allowance\");\n        if (allowed != type(uint256).max) {\n            allowance[from][msg.sender] = allowed - amount;\n        }\n        _transfer(from, to, amount);\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    function _transfer(address from, address to, uint256 amount) internal {\n        require(balanceOf[from] >= amount, \"insufficient balance\");\n        balanceOf[from] -= amount;\n        balanceOf[to] += amount;\n        emit Transfer(from, to, amount);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1850,"cache_read_input_tokens":19741,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1850},"output_tokens":14,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1c398c09-4d5b-4f28-a0e9-8dda39bfa970","uuid":"c01e0734-87a9-4a02-bb67-a1d4c6dc8fc7","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:37:45.057Z","request_id":"req_011CeD4msGTaVAjzRfKGqLDY"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD4nfQrWmnNZiCeN3eiu","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01TuyQFTShouZXrzHGTpGT56","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-20T003606Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/contracts/script/DeployLocal.s.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.24;\n\nimport {Script, console} from \"forge-std/Script.sol\";\nimport {TipJar} from \"../src/TipJar.sol\";\nimport {MockUSDC} from \"../src/MockUSDC.sol\";\n\n/// @notice Local deployment: deploys a MockUSDC, the TipJar pointed at it, and\n///         mints test USDC to the default Anvil accounts. Writes the resulting\n///         addresses to ../frontend/deployment.local.json for the web app.\ncontract DeployLocal is Script {\n    // Default Anvil accounts (well-known test keys, safe for local dev only).\n    address constant ANVIL_0 = 0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266;\n    address constant ANVIL_1 = 0x70997970C51812dc3A010C7d01b50e0d17dc79C8;\n    address constant ANVIL_2 = 0x3C44CdDdB6a900fa2b585dd299e03d12FA4293BC;\n\n    function run() external {\n        uint256 pk = vm.envOr(\n            \"PRIVATE_KEY\",\n            uint256(0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80)\n        );\n\n        vm.startBroadcast(pk);\n\n        MockUSDC usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        TipJar tipJar = new TipJar(address(usdc));\n\n        // 1,000,000 USDC (6 decimals) to each test account so tips can be sent.\n        uint256 seed = 1_000_000 * 1e6;\n        usdc.mint(ANVIL_0, seed);\n        usdc.mint(ANVIL_1, seed);\n        usdc.mint(ANVIL_2, seed);\n\n        vm.stopBroadcast();\n\n        console.log(\"MockUSDC:\", address(usdc));\n        console.log(\"TipJar:  \", address(tipJar));\n\n        string memory json = string.concat(\n            \"{\\n\",\n            '  \"chainId\": 31337,\\n',\n            '  \"usdc\": \"',\n            vm.toString(address(usdc)),\n            '\",\\n',\n            '  \"tipJar\": \"',\n            vm.toString(address(tipJar)),\n            '\"\\n',\n            \"}\\n\"\n        );\n        vm.writeFile(\"../frontend/deployment.local.json\", json);\n        console.log(\"Wrote ../frontend/deployment.local.json\");\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1056,"cache_read_input_tokens":21591,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1056},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1c398c09-4d5b-4f28-a0e9-8dda39bfa970","uuid":"d9c97157-2b1c-4a4d-9f55-143e512a8260","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:38:05.705Z","request_id":"req_011CeD4nea1FpmvA7kU7NVbS"}

Writes the resulting\n///         addresses to ../frontend/deployment.local.json for the web app.\ncontract DeployLocal is Script {\n    // Default Anvil accounts (well-known test keys, safe for local dev only).\n    address constant ANVIL_0 = 0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266;\n    address constant ANVIL_1 = 0x70997970C51812dc3A010C7d01b50e0d17dc79C8;\n    address constant ANVIL_2 = 0x3C44CdDdB6a900fa2b585dd299e03d12FA4293BC;\n\n    function run() external {\n        uint256 pk = vm.envOr(\n            \"PRIVATE_KEY\",\n            uint256(0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80)\n        );\n\n        vm.startBroadcast(pk);\n\n        MockUSDC usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        TipJar tipJar = new TipJar(address(usdc));\n\n        // 1,000,000 USDC (6 decimals) to each test account so tips can be sent.\n        uint256 seed = 1_000_000 * 1e6;\n        usdc.mint(ANVIL_0, seed);\n        usdc.mint(ANVIL_1, seed);\n        usdc.mint(ANVIL_2, seed);\n\n        vm.stopBroadcast();\n\n        console.log(\"MockUSDC:\", address(usdc));\n        console.log(\"TipJar:  \", address(tipJar));\n\n        string memory json = string.concat(\n            \"{\\n\",\n            '  \"chainId\": 31337,\\n',\n            '  \"usdc\": \"',\n            vm.toString(address(usdc)),\n            '\",\\n',\n            '  \"tipJar\": \"',\n            vm.toString(address(tipJar)),\n            '\"\\n',\n            \"}\\n\"\n        );\n        vm.writeFile(\"../frontend/deployment.local.json\", json);\n        console.log(\"Wrote ../frontend/deployment.local.json\");\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD4pBcLD5Zn2VxpuvwME","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01VThSNmHWSU2MToPbEkq2F8","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-20T003606Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/contracts/test/TipJar.t.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.24;\n\nimport {Test} from \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\nimport {TipJar} from \"../src/TipJar.sol\";\nimport {MockUSDC} from \"../src/MockUSDC.sol\";\n\ncontract TipJarTest is Test {\n    MockUSDC usdc;\n    TipJar jar;\n\n    address owner = address(this);\n    address alice = makeAddr(\"alice\");\n    address bob = makeAddr(\"bob\");\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        jar = new TipJar(address(usdc));\n        usdc.mint(alice, 1_000e6);\n        usdc.mint(bob, 1_000e6);\n    }\n\n    function _tip(address from, uint256 amount, string memory msg_) internal {\n        vm.startPrank(from);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), amount);\n        jar.tip(amount, msg_);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_TipRecordsFeedAndTransfers() public {\n        _tip(alice, 10e6, \"gm\");\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(jar)), 10e6);\n        assertEq(jar.balance(), 10e6);\n        assertEq(jar.tipsCount(), 1);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 10e6);\n        assertEq(jar.tippedBy(alice), 10e6);\n\n        TipJar.Tip memory t = jar.getTip(0);\n        assertEq(t.sender, alice);\n        assertEq(t.amount, 10e6);\n        assertEq(t.message, \"gm\");\n    }\n\n    function test_RecentTipsAreNewestFirst() public {\n        _tip(alice, 1e6, \"first\");\n        _tip(bob, 2e6, \"second\");\n        _tip(alice, 3e6, \"third\");\n\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory recent = jar.getRecentTips(2);\n        assertEq(recent.length, 2);\n        assertEq(recent[0].message, \"third\");\n        assertEq(recent[1].message, \"second\");\n    }\n\n    function test_RecentTipsClampsToTotal() public {\n        _tip(alice, 1e6, \"only\");\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory recent = jar.getRecentTips(50);\n        assertEq(recent.length, 1);\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertOnZeroAmount() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAmount.selector);\n        jar.tip(0, \"nope\");\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertWithoutApproval() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(); // MockUSDC reverts \"insufficient allowance\"\n        jar.tip(5e6, \"no approve\");\n    }\n\n    function test_OwnerCanWithdraw() public {\n        _tip(alice, 100e6, \"thanks\");\n        uint256 before = usdc.balanceOf(owner);\n        jar.withdraw(40e6);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), before + 40e6);\n        assertEq(jar.balance(), 60e6);\n    }\n\n    function test_NonOwnerCannotWithdraw() public {\n        _tip(alice, 100e6, \"thanks\");\n        vm.prank(bob);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NotOwner.selector);\n        jar.withdraw(1e6);\n    }\n\n    function test_ConstructorRejectsZeroToken() public {\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAddress.selector);\n        new TipJar(address(0));\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1445,"cache_read_input_tokens":22647,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1445},"output_tokens":14,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1c398c09-4d5b-4f28-a0e9-8dda39bfa970","uuid":"e26b4819-761e-4482-a9fa-bfbdac169dca","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:38:19.716Z","request_id":"req_011CeD4pAsBbhWcj6KUaiHA2"}

makeAddr(\"bob\");\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        jar = new TipJar(address(usdc));\n        usdc.mint(alice, 1_000e6);\n        usdc.mint(bob, 1_000e6);\n    }\n\n    function _tip(address from, uint256 amount, string memory msg_) internal {\n        vm.startPrank(from);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), amount);\n        jar.tip(amount, msg_);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_TipRecordsFeedAndTransfers() public {\n        _tip(alice, 10e6, \"gm\");\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(address(jar)), 10e6);\n        assertEq(jar.balance(), 10e6);\n        assertEq(jar.tipsCount(), 1);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTipped(), 10e6);\n        assertEq(jar.tippedBy(alice), 10e6);\n\n        TipJar.Tip memory t = jar.getTip(0);\n        assertEq(t.sender, alice);\n        assertEq(t.amount, 10e6);\n        assertEq(t.message, \"gm\");\n    }\n\n    function test_RecentTipsAreNewestFirst() public {\n        _tip(alice, 1e6, \"first\");\n        _tip(bob, 2e6, \"second\");\n        _tip(alice, 3e6, \"third\");\n\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory recent = jar.getRecentTips(2);\n        assertEq(recent.length, 2);\n        assertEq(recent[0].message, \"third\");\n        assertEq(recent[1].message, \"second\");\n    }\n\n    function test_RecentTipsClampsToTotal() public {\n        _tip(alice, 1e6, \"only\");\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory recent = jar.getRecentTips(50);\n        assertEq(recent.length, 1);\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertOnZeroAmount() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAmount.selector);\n        jar.tip(0, \"nope\");\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertWithoutApproval() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(); // MockUSDC reverts \"insufficient allowance\"\n        jar.tip(5e6, \"no approve\");\n    }\n\n    function test_OwnerCanWithdraw() public {\n        _tip(alice, 100e6, \"thanks\");\n        uint256 before = usdc.balanceOf(owner);\n        jar.withdraw(40e6);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), before + 40e6);\n        assertEq(jar.balance(), 60e6);\n    }\n\n    function test_NonOwnerCannotWithdraw() public {\n        _tip(alice, 100e6, \"thanks\");\n        vm.prank(bob);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NotOwner.selector);\n        jar.withdraw(1e6);\n    }\n\n    function test_ConstructorRejectsZeroToken() public {\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAddress.selector);\n        new TipJar(address(0));\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD4vhrvSuNXQCgjiS2cs","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01FnTcZS96TN2huQfwS92VuV","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-20T003606Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/frontend/scripts/sync-deployment.mjs","content":"// Reads deployment.local.json (written by the Foundry deploy script) and writes\n// the addresses into .env.local so the Next.js app can read them at build time.\n// Safe to run anytime; if no deployment file exists it just leaves things be.\nimport { readFileSync, writeFileSync, existsSync } from \"node:fs\";\nimport { fileURLToPath } from \"node:url\";\nimport { dirname, join } from \"node:path\";\n\nconst here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));\nconst root = join(here, \"..\");\nconst deploymentPath = join(root, \"deployment.local.json\");\nconst envPath = join(root, \".env.local\");\n\nif (!existsSync(deploymentPath)) {\n  console.log(\n    \"[sync-deployment] No deployment.local.json found — run the contract deploy first.\\n\" +\n      \"                  Falling back to whatever is already in .env.local.\"\n  );\n  process.exit(0);\n}\n\nconst { chainId, usdc, tipJar } = JSON.parse(readFileSync(deploymentPath, \"utf8\"));\n\nconst lines = [\n  \"# Auto-generated by scripts/sync-deployment.mjs — do not edit by hand.\",\n  `NEXT_PUBLIC_CHAIN_ID=${chainId}`,\n  `NEXT_PUBLIC_USDC_ADDRESS=${usdc}`,\n  `NEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS=${tipJar}`,\n  `NEXT_PUBLIC_RPC_URL=${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_RPC_URL ?? if no deployment file exists it just leaves things be.\nimport { readFileSync, writeFileSync, existsSync } from \"node:fs\";\nimport { fileURLToPath } from \"node:url\";\nimport { dirname, join } from \"node:path\";\n\nconst here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));\nconst root = join(here, \"..\");\nconst deploymentPath = join(root, \"deployment.local.json\");\nconst envPath = join(root, \".env.local\");\n\nif (!existsSync(deploymentPath)) {\n  console.log(\n    \"[sync-deployment] No deployment.local.json found — run the contract deploy first.\\n\" +\n      \"                  Falling back to whatever is already in .env.local.\"\n  );\n  process.exit(0);\n}\n\nconst { chainId, usdc, tipJar } = JSON.parse(readFileSync(deploymentPath, \"utf8\"));\n\nconst lines = [\n  \"# Auto-generated by scripts/sync-deployment.mjs — do not edit by hand.\",\n  `NEXT_PUBLIC_CHAIN_ID=${chainId}`,\n  `NEXT_PUBLIC_USDC_ADDRESS=${usdc}`,\n  `NEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS=${tipJar}`,\n  `NEXT_PUBLIC_RPC_URL=${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_RPC_URL ?? \"http://127.0.0.1:8545\"}`,\n  \"\",\n];\n\nwriteFileSync(envPath, lines.join(\"\\n\"));\nconsole.log(`[sync-deployment] Wrote .env.local (chainId=${chainId}, tipJar=${tipJar}).`);\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

 padding: 12px;\n  font-size: 15px;\n  font-family: inherit;\n}\n\ntextarea {\n  resize: vertical;\n  min-height: 64px;\n}\n\n.row {\n  margin-bottom: 14px;\n}\n\nbutton {\n  background: {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD52NyHET4wTtsHRcqCb","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01927UrrM5k9MDf1bp8LwLRk","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-20T003606Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/frontend/components/Stats.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useReadContracts } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { formatUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { TIPJAR_ADDRESS, tipJarAbi, USDC_DECIMALS } from \"@/lib/contracts\";\n\nfunction usd(v: bigint | undefined) {\n  if (v === undefined) return \"—\";\n  const n = Number(formatUnits(v, USDC_DECIMALS));\n  return n.toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 2 });\n}\n\nexport function Stats({ refreshKey }: { refreshKey: number }) {\n  const { data } = useReadContracts({\n    contracts: [\n      { address: TIPJAR_ADDRESS, abi: tipJarAbi, functionName: \"totalTipped\" },\n      { address: TIPJAR_ADDRESS, abi: tipJarAbi, functionName: \"tipsCount\" },\n      { address: TIPJAR_ADDRESS, abi: tipJarAbi, functionName: \"balance\" },\n    ],\n    query: { enabled: Boolean(TIPJAR_ADDRESS), refetchInterval: 5000 },\n    // refreshKey forces a re-render/refetch after a new tip is sent.\n    scopeKey: `stats-${refreshKey}`,\n  });\n\n  const total = data?.[0]?.result as bigint | undefined;\n  const count = data?.[1]?.result as bigint | undefined;\n  const bal = data?.[2]?.result as bigint | undefined;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"stats\">\n      <div className=\"stat\">\n        <div className=\"label\">Total tipped</div>\n        <div className=\"value\">${usd(total)}</div>\n      </div>\n      <div className=\"stat\">\n        <div className=\"label\">Tips</div>\n        <div className=\"value\">{count === undefined ? \"—\" : count.toString()}</div>\n      </div>\n      <div className=\"stat\">\n        <div className=\"label\">Jar balance</div>\n        <div className=\"value\">${usd(bal)}</div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":732,"cache_read_input_tokens":38625,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":732},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1c398c09-4d5b-4f28-a0e9-8dda39bfa970","uuid":"8c7f52d5-be4a-4841-a07f-b2ca412a198c","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:41:01.710Z","request_id":"req_011CeD52NFsjP9sawfpTNfkf"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD53AB8AAzAGwFzvux4f","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_015ozjSZXPVfA3csswM8UXpi","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-20T003606Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/frontend/components/TipFeed.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useReadContract } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { formatUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { TIPJAR_ADDRESS, tipJarAbi, USDC_DECIMALS, type Tip } from \"@/lib/contracts\";\n\nfunction short(addr: string) {\n  return `${addr.slice(0, 6)}…${addr.slice(-4)}`;\n}\n\nfunction timeAgo(ts: bigint) {\n  const secs = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) - Number(ts);\n  if (secs < 60) return `${Math.max(secs, 0)}s ago`;\n  if (secs < 3600) return `${Math.floor(secs / 60)}m ago`;\n  if (secs < 86400) return `${Math.floor(secs / 3600)}h ago`;\n  return `${Math.floor(secs / 86400)}d ago`;\n}\n\nexport function TipFeed({ refreshKey }: { refreshKey: number }) {\n  const { data, isLoading } = useReadContract({\n    address: TIPJAR_ADDRESS,\n    abi: tipJarAbi,\n    functionName: \"getRecentTips\",\n    args: [50n],\n    query: {\n      enabled: Boolean(TIPJAR_ADDRESS),\n      refetchInterval: 5000,\n      scopeKey: `feed-${refreshKey}`,\n    },\n  });\n\n  const tips = (data ?? []) as readonly Tip[];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card\">\n      <h2>Recent tips</h2>\n      {!TIPJAR_ADDRESS ? (\n        <p className=\"empty\">No TipJar address configured. Run the deploy + sync steps.</p>\n      ) : isLoading ? (\n        <p className=\"empty\">Loading feed…</p>\n      ) : tips.length === 0 ? (\n        <p className=\"empty\">No tips yet. Be the first to drop some USDC! 🫗</p>\n      ) : (\n        tips.map((t, i) => (\n          <div className=\"feed-item\" key={`${t.sender}-${t.timestamp}-${i}`}>\n            <div>\n              <div className=\"who\">{short(t.sender)}</div>\n              {t.message ? <p className=\"msg\">{t.message}</p> : <p className=\"msg muted\">(no message)</p>}\n              <div className=\"time\">{timeAgo(t.timestamp)}</div>\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"amt\">\n              ${Number(formatUnits(t.amount, USDC_DECIMALS)).toLocaleString(undefined, {\n                maximumFractionDigits: 2,\n              })}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        ))\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1115,"cache_read_input_tokens":39357,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1115},"output_tokens":20,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1c398c09-4d5b-4f28-a0e9-8dda39bfa970","uuid":"d583a9b6-0db2-4f89-8d23-8dca24f51135","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:41:12.335Z","request_id":"req_011CeD539GZ9phx3YGrmGVfy"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD53wiJSsAK5W6Mi5nAt","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01X1W2mENqL5Y2juZYvsCnPv","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-20T003606Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/frontend/components/TipForm.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport {\n  useAccount,\n  useReadContract,\n  useWriteContractAsync,\n  usePublicClient,\n} from \"wagmi\";\nimport { parseUnits, formatUnits, maxUint256 } from \"viem\";\nimport {\n  TIPJAR_ADDRESS,\n  USDC_ADDRESS,\n  USDC_DECIMALS,\n  tipJarAbi,\n  erc20Abi,\n} from \"@/lib/contracts\";\n\nconst IS_LOCAL = Number(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_CHAIN_ID ?? 31337) !== 8453;\n\nexport function TipForm({ onTipped }: { onTipped: () => void }) {\n  const { address, isConnected } = useAccount();\n  const publicClient = usePublicClient();\n  const { writeContractAsync } = useWriteContractAsync();\n\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"1\");\n  const [message, setMessage] = useState(\"\");\n  const [status, setStatus] = useState<string>(\"\");\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string>(\"\");\n  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);\n\n  const { data: balance, refetch: refetchBalance } = useReadContract({\n    address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n    abi: erc20Abi,\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: address ? [address] : undefined,\n    query: { enabled: Boolean(address) },\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance, refetch: refetchAllowance } = useReadContract({\n    address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n    abi: erc20Abi,\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: address ? [address, TIPJAR_ADDRESS] : undefined,\n    query: { enabled: Boolean(address) },\n  });\n\n  async function handleTip(e: React.FormEvent) {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    setError(\"\");\n    setStatus(\"\");\n\n    if (!publicClient) return;\n    let value: bigint;\n    try {\n      value = parseUnits(amount || \"0\", USDC_DECIMALS);\n    } catch {\n      setError(\"Invalid amount\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (value <= 0n) {\n      setError(\"Enter an amount greater than 0\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    setBusy(true);\n    try {\n      // Step 1: approve the TipJar to pull USDC, if the allowance is too low.\n      const current = (allowance as bigint | undefined) ?? 0n;\n      if (current < value) {\n        setStatus(\"Approving USDC…\");\n        const approveHash = await writeContractAsync({\n          address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n          abi: erc20Abi,\n          functionName: \"approve\",\n          args: [TIPJAR_ADDRESS, maxUint256],\n        });\n        await publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: approveHash });\n        await refetchAllowance();\n      }\n\n      // Step 2: send the tip.\n      setStatus(\"Sending tip…\");\n      const tipHash = await writeContractAsync({\n        address: TIPJAR_ADDRESS,\n        abi: tipJarAbi,\n        functionName: \"tip\",\n        args: [value, message],\n      });\n      await publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: tipHash });\n\n      setStatus(\"Tip sent! 🎉\");\n      setMessage(\"\");\n      await Promise.all([refetchBalance(), refetchAllowance()]);\n      onTipped();\n    } catch (err) {\n      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message.split(\"\\n\")[0] : \"Transaction failed\");\n      setStatus(\"\");\n    } finally {\n      setBusy(false);\n    }\n  }\n\n  async function mintTestUsdc() {\n    if (!publicClient || !address) return;\n    setError(\"\");\n    setStatus(\"Minting 1,000 test USDC…\");\n    setBusy(true);\n    try {\n      const hash = await writeContractAsync({\n        address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n        abi: erc20Abi,\n        functionName: \"mint\",\n        args: [address, parseUnits(\"1000\", USDC_DECIMALS)],\n      });\n      await publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash });\n      await refetchBalance();\n      setStatus(\"Minted 1,000 test USDC.\");\n    } catch (err) {\n      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message.split(\"\\n\")[0] : \"Mint failed\");\n      setStatus(\"\");\n    } finally {\n      setBusy(false);\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (!isConnected) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"card\">\n        <h2>Send a tip</h2>\n        <p className=\"muted\">Connect your wallet to send a USDC tip.</p>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card\">\n      <h2>Send a tip</h2>\n      <form onSubmit={handleTip}>\n        <div className=\"row\">\n          <label htmlFor=\"amount\">Amount (USDC)</label>\n          <input\n            id=\"amount\"\n            inputMode=\"decimal\"\n            value={amount}\n            onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            placeholder=\"1.00\"\n          />\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"row\">\n          <label htmlFor=\"message\">Message (optional)</label>\n          <textarea\n            id=\"message\"\n            value={message}\n            onChange={(e) => setMessage(e.target.value)}\n            placeholder=\"gm, thanks for the great work!\"\n            maxLength={280}\n          />\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"inline\">\n          <button type=\"submit\" disabled={busy}>\n            {busy ? \"Working…\" : \"Send tip\"}\n          </button>\n          {IS_LOCAL ? (\n            <button type=\"button\" className=\"ghost\" onClick={mintTestUsdc} disabled={busy}>\n              Get 1,000 test USDC\n            </button>\n          ) : null}\n        </div>\n      </form>\n      <p className=\"muted\" style={{ marginTop: 12 }}>\n        Balance:{\" \"}\n        {balance === undefined\n          ? \"—\"\n          : Number(formatUnits(balance as bigint, USDC_DECIMALS)).toLocaleString(undefined, {\n              maximumFractionDigits: 2,\n            })}{\" \"}\n        USDC\n      </p>\n      {status ? <p className=\"success\">{status}</p> : null}\n      {error ? <p className=\"error\">{error}</p> : null}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1187,"cache_read_input_tokens":40472,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1187},"output_tokens":7,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1c398c09-4d5b-4f28-a0e9-8dda39bfa970","uuid":"9b97db5f-eef6-48cb-8bbe-04e0e7698781","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:41:41.259Z","request_id":"req_011CeD53vkGDcfQz7sVttcSB"}

(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1c398c09-4d5b-4f28-a0e9-8dda39bfa970","uuid":"38453ebf-b10a-4c69-a407-faae4da0c713","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:44:06.217Z","tool_use_result":{"filePath":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-20T003606Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/frontend/lib/wagmi.ts","oldString":"import { injected } from \"wagmi/connectors\";","newString":"// Import from @wagmi/core rather than the wagmi/connectors barrel: the barrel\n// eagerly references Coinbase's baseAccount connector and its optional deps\n// (@x402/*), which we don't use and which break the build.\nimport { injected } from \"@wagmi/core\";","originalFile":"import { createConfig, http } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { base } from \"wagmi/chains\";\nimport { defineChain } from \"viem\";\nimport { injected } from \"wagmi/connectors\";\n\nconst CHAIN_ID = Number(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_CHAIN_ID ?? 31337);\nconst RPC_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_RPC_URL ?? \"http://127.0.0.1:8545\";\n\n// Local Anvil chain (also used for a Base fork, which runs on 31337 in our setup).\nconst localChain = defineChain({\n  id: CHAIN_ID,\n  name: \"Local Anvil\",\n  nativeCurrency: { name: \"Ether\", symbol: \"ETH\", decimals: 18 },\n  rpcUrls: { default: { http: [RPC_URL] } },\n});\n\n// Use the real Base chain when the app is pointed at chain 8453, otherwise the\n// local Anvil chain. Only one is active at a time, driven by NEXT_PUBLIC_CHAIN_ID.\nexport const activeChain = CHAIN_ID === base.id ? base : localChain;\n\nexport const wagmiConfig = createConfig({\n  chains: [activeChain],\n  connectors: [injected()],\n  transports: {\n    [activeChain.id]: http(RPC_URL),\n  },\n  ssr: true,\n});\n\ndeclare module \"wagmi\" {\n  interface Register {\n    config: typeof wagmiConfig;\n  }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":7,"newStart":1,"newLines":10,"lines":[" import { createConfig, http } from \"wagmi\";"," import { base } from \"wagmi/chains\";"," import { defineChain } from \"viem\";","-import { injected } from \"wagmi/connectors\";","+// Import from @wagmi/core rather than the wagmi/connectors barrel: the barrel","+// eagerly references Coinbase's baseAccount connector and its optional deps","+// (@x402/*), which we don't use and which break the build.","+import { injected } from \"@wagmi/core\";"," "," const CHAIN_ID = Number(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_CHAIN_ID ?? 31337);"," const RPC_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_RPC_URL ?? \"http://127.0.0.1:8545\";"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

[address] : undefined,\n    query: { enabled: Boolean(address) },\n  });\n\n  const { data: allowance, refetch: refetchAllowance } = useReadContract({\n    address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n    abi: erc20Abi,\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: address ? [address, TIPJAR_ADDRESS] : undefined,\n    query: { enabled: Boolean(address) },\n  });\n\n  async function handleTip(e: React.FormEvent) {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    setError(\"\");\n    setStatus(\"\");\n\n    if (!publicClient) return;\n    let value: bigint;\n    try {\n      value = parseUnits(amount || \"0\", USDC_DECIMALS);\n    } catch {\n      setError(\"Invalid amount\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (value <= 0n) {\n      setError(\"Enter an amount greater than 0\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    setBusy(true);\n    try {\n      // Step 1: approve the TipJar to pull USDC, if the allowance is too low.\n      const current = (allowance as bigint | undefined) ?? 0n;\n      if (current < value) {\n        setStatus(\"Approving USDC…\");\n        const approveHash = await writeContractAsync({\n          address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n          abi: erc20Abi,\n          functionName: \"approve\",\n          args: [TIPJAR_ADDRESS, maxUint256],\n        });\n        await publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: approveHash });\n        await refetchAllowance();\n      }\n\n      // Step 2: send the tip.\n      setStatus(\"Sending tip…\");\n      const tipHash = await writeContractAsync({\n        address: TIPJAR_ADDRESS,\n        abi: tipJarAbi,\n        functionName: \"tip\",\n        args: [value, message],\n      });\n      await publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: tipHash });\n\n      setStatus(\"Tip sent! 🎉\");\n      setMessage(\"\");\n      await Promise.all([refetchBalance(), refetchAllowance()]);\n      onTipped();\n    } catch (err) {\n      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message.split(\"\\n\")[0] : \"Transaction failed\");\n      setStatus(\"\");\n    } finally {\n      setBusy(false);\n    }\n  }\n\n  async function mintTestUsdc() {\n    if (!publicClient || !address) return;\n    setError(\"\");\n    setStatus(\"Minting 1,000 test USDC…\");\n    setBusy(true);\n    try {\n      const hash = await writeContractAsync({\n        address: USDC_ADDRESS,\n        abi: erc20Abi,\n        functionName: \"mint\",\n        args: [address, parseUnits(\"1000\", USDC_DECIMALS)],\n      });\n      await publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash });\n      await refetchBalance();\n      setStatus(\"Minted 1,000 test USDC.\");\n    } catch (err) {\n      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message.split(\"\\n\")[0] : \"Mint failed\");\n      setStatus(\"\");\n    } finally {\n      setBusy(false);\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (!isConnected) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"card\">\n        <h2>Send a tip</h2>\n        <p className=\"muted\">Connect your wallet to send a USDC tip.</p>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card\">\n      <h2>Send a tip</h2>\n      <form onSubmit={handleTip}>\n        <div className=\"row\">\n          <label htmlFor=\"amount\">Amount (USDC)</label>\n          <input\n            id=\"amount\"\n            inputMode=\"decimal\"\n            value={amount}\n            onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)}\n            placeholder=\"1.00\"\n          />\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"row\">\n          <label htmlFor=\"message\">Message (optional)</label>\n          <textarea\n            id=\"message\"\n            value={message}\n            onChange={(e) => setMessage(e.target.value)}\n            placeholder=\"gm, thanks for the great work!\"\n            maxLength={280}\n          />\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"inline\">\n          <button type=\"submit\" disabled={busy}>\n            {busy ? \"Working…\" : \"Send tip\"}\n          </button>\n          {IS_LOCAL ? (\n            <button type=\"button\" className=\"ghost\" onClick={mintTestUsdc} disabled={busy}>\n              Get 1,000 test USDC\n            </button>\n          ) : null}\n        </div>\n      </form>\n      <p className=\"muted\" style={{ marginTop: 12 }}>\n        Balance:{\" \"}\n        {balance === undefined\n          ? \"—\"\n          : Number(formatUnits(balance as bigint, USDC_DECIMALS)).toLocaleString(undefined, {\n              maximumFractionDigits: 2,\n            })}{\" \"}\n        USDC\n      </p>\n      {status ? <p className=\"success\">{status}</p> : null}\n      {error ? <p className=\"error\">{error}</p> : null}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":12,"newStart":1,"newLines":7,"lines":[" \"use client\";"," "," import { useState } from \"react\";","-import {","-  useAccount,","-  useReadContract,","-  useWriteContractAsync,","-  usePublicClient,","-} from \"wagmi\";","+import { useAccount, useReadContract, useWriteContract, usePublicClient } from \"wagmi\";"," import { parseUnits, formatUnits, maxUint256 } from \"viem\";"," import {","   TIPJAR_ADDRESS,"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

[]) as readonly Tip[];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card\">\n      <h2>Recent tips</h2>\n      {!TIPJAR_ADDRESS ? (\n        <p className=\"empty\">No TipJar address configured. Run the deploy + sync steps.</p>\n      ) : isLoading ? (\n        <p className=\"empty\">Loading feed…</p>\n      ) : tips.length === 0 ? (\n        <p className=\"empty\">No tips yet. Be the first to drop some USDC! 🫗</p>\n      ) : (\n        tips.map((t, i) => (\n          <div className=\"feed-item\" key={`${t.sender}-${t.timestamp}-${i}`}>\n            <div>\n              <div className=\"who\">{short(t.sender)}</div>\n              {t.message ? <p className=\"msg\">{t.message}</p> : <p className=\"msg muted\">(no message)</p>}\n              <div className=\"time\">{timeAgo(t.timestamp)}</div>\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"amt\">\n              ${Number(formatUnits(t.amount, USDC_DECIMALS)).toLocaleString(undefined, {\n                maximumFractionDigits: 2,\n              })}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        ))\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":22,"oldLines":10,"newStart":22,"newLines":10,"lines":["     abi: tipJarAbi,","     functionName: \"getRecentTips\",","     args: [50n],","+    scopeKey: `feed-${refreshKey}`,","     query: {","       enabled: Boolean(TIPJAR_ADDRESS),","       refetchInterval: 5000,","-      scopeKey: `feed-${refreshKey}`,","     },","   });"," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD5UoQgRapneAx2QVPby","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01TghGDCpVTsjfAttRxTQqpc","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-20T003606Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/README.md","content":"# USDC Tip Jar (Base)\n\nA minimal, full-stack **USDC tip jar** for [Base](https://base.org):\n\n- **Onchain contract** (`TipJar.sol`) that accepts USDC tips, attaches a short\n  message to each, and keeps an onchain feed plus running totals.\n- **Web app** (Next.js + wagmi + viem) with a **connect-wallet flow**, a **live\n  tip feed**, and a **form to send a tip** (handles the USDC `approve` → `tip`\n  two-step for you).\n\nEverything runs **locally** against an [Anvil](https://book.getfoundry.sh/anvil/)\nchain. A tiny `MockUSDC` (6-decimal ERC-20) stands in for USDC so you don't need\nany real funds or a testnet — no deployment to any live network is required.\n\n> On real Base, the same `TipJar` is simply constructed with the canonical USDC\n> address `0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`. This project deliberately\n> **does not deploy anywhere** — see [Pointing at real Base](#pointing-at-real-base).\n\n---\n\n## Project layout\n\n```\n.\n├── contracts/                 # Foundry project (Solidity)\n│   ├── src/\n│   │   ├── TipJar.sol         # the tip jar\n│   │   ├── IERC20.sol         # minimal ERC-20 interface\n│   │   └── MockUSDC.sol       # local-only 6-decimal USDC stand-in\n│   ├── script/DeployLocal.s.sol\n│   └── test/TipJar.t.sol      # 8 unit tests\n└── frontend/                  # Next.js app (App Router)\n    ├── app/                   # layout, page, providers\n    ├── components/            # ConnectWallet, TipForm, TipFeed, Stats\n    ├── lib/                   # wagmi config, contract ABIs + addresses\n    └── scripts/sync-deployment.mjs\n```\n\n---\n\n## Prerequisites\n\n- **Node.js 18+** and npm\n- **Foundry** (`forge`, `anvil`, `cast`) — install:\n  ```bash\n  curl -L https://foundry.paradigm.xyz | bash && foundryup\n  ```\n- A browser wallet (e.g. **MetaMask**) for the connect-wallet flow.\n\n---\n\n## Quick start\n\nYou'll use **three terminals**: one for the local chain, one to deploy the\ncontracts, one for the web app.\n\n### 1. Start a local chain (Terminal A)\n\n```bash\nanvil\n```\n\nLeave it running. It listens on `http://127.0.0.1:8545` (chain id `31337`) and\nprints 10 pre-funded test accounts with their private keys.\n\n### 2. Deploy the contracts (Terminal B)\n\n```bash\ncd contracts\nforge install foundry-rs/forge-std --no-git   # first time only, fetches forge-std\nforge test                                     # optional: run the unit tests\nforge script script/DeployLocal.s.sol:DeployLocal \\\n  --rpc-url http://127.0.0.1:8545 --broadcast\n```\n\nThis deploys `MockUSDC` + `TipJar`, mints 1,000,000 test USDC to the first three\nAnvil accounts, and writes the addresses to `frontend/deployment.local.json`.\n\n### 3. Run the web app (Terminal C)\n\n```bash\ncd frontend\nnpm install\nnpm run dev\n```\n\n`npm run dev` first runs `sync-deployment` (which turns\n`deployment.local.json` into `frontend/.env.local`), then starts Next.js at\n**http://localhost:3000**.\n\n### 4. Connect a wallet and tip\n\n1. In MetaMask, add the local network:\n   - **Network name:** Anvil Local\n   - **RPC URL:** `http://127.0.0.1:8545`\n   - **Chain ID:** `31337`\n   - **Currency:** `ETH`\n2. Import one of the Anvil test accounts (copy a private key from Terminal A).\n   The default first account is:\n   - Address `0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266`\n   - Key `0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80`\n   > These are Anvil's well-known public test keys — **never** use them on a real\n   > network.\n3. Open http://localhost:3000, click **Connect Wallet**.\n4. Click **Get 1,000 test USDC** (mints from `MockUSDC` — only shown in local\n   mode), then enter an amount + message and hit **Send tip**. Approve the two\n   prompts (USDC approval, then the tip). The feed and stats update immediately.\n\n---\n\n## The contract\n\n`TipJar` (`contracts/src/TipJar.sol`):\n\n- `tip(uint256 amount, string message)` — pulls `amount` USDC from the caller via\n  `transferFrom` (caller must `approve` first), records `{sender, amount,\n  timestamp, message}` in the feed, and emits `NewTip`.\n- `getRecentTips(uint256 limit)` — the feed, **newest first**.\n- `tipsCount()`, `totalTipped()`, `tippedBy(address)`, `balance()` — read helpers\n  the UI uses for its stat tiles.\n- `withdraw(uint256)` / `transferOwnership(address)` — owner-only.\n\nThe USDC address is an immutable constructor argument, so the exact same contract\nworks against `MockUSDC` locally and real USDC on Base.\n\nRun the tests anytime:\n\n```bash\ncd contracts && forge test -vvv\n```\n\n---\n\n## Pointing at real Base\n\nThis exercise does **not** deploy anywhere. If you later wanted to run the UI\nagainst real Base (after deploying `TipJar` yourself with the real USDC address),\nset these in `frontend/.env.local` and restart `next dev`:\n\n```bash\nNEXT_PUBLIC_CHAIN_ID=8453\nNEXT_PUBLIC_RPC_URL=https://mainnet.base.org\nNEXT_PUBLIC_USDC_ADDRESS=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\nNEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS=<your deployed TipJar>\n```\n\nIn this mode the \"Get test USDC\" button disappears (real USDC has no `mint`), and\nyou'd fund your wallet with actual USDC. See `frontend/.env.local.example`.\n\n---\n\n## Troubleshooting\n\n- **\"No TipJar address configured\"** in the UI — you skipped the deploy, or\n  `deployment.local.json` wasn't synced. Re-run the deploy (step 2), then\n  `cd frontend && npm run sync-deployment` and restart the dev server.\n- **Feed is empty** — that's expected before the first tip. Send one.\n- **Wallet on the wrong network** — make sure MetaMask is on chain id `31337`.\n- **Restarted Anvil** — its state resets, so re-run the deploy script and\n  `npm run sync-deployment` (addresses will be the same on a fresh chain).\n```\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":669,"cache_read_input_tokens":58558,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":669},"output_tokens":17,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1c398c09-4d5b-4f28-a0e9-8dda39bfa970","uuid":"efc25bf7-c7fd-4850-8e61-2b786a5f555f","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:47:24.919Z","request_id":"req_011CeD5UmHwRdiJqhNxkLhPP"}

Connect a wallet and tip\n\n1. In MetaMask, add the local network:\n   - **Network name:** Anvil Local\n   - **RPC URL:** `http://127.0.0.1:8545`\n   - **Chain ID:** `31337`\n   - **Currency:** `ETH`\n2. Import one of the Anvil test accounts (copy a private key from Terminal A).\n   The default first account is:\n   - Address `0xf39Fd6e51aad88F6F4ce6aB8827279cffFb92266`\n   - Key `0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80`\n   > These are Anvil's well-known public test keys — **never** use them on a real\n   > network.\n3. Open http://localhost:3000, click **Connect Wallet**.\n4. Click **Get 1,000 test USDC** (mints from `MockUSDC` — only shown in local\n   mode), then enter an amount + message and hit **Send tip**. Approve the two\n   prompts (USDC approval, then the tip). The feed and stats update immediately.\n\n---\n\n## The contract\n\n`TipJar` (`contracts/src/TipJar.sol`):\n\n- `tip(uint256 amount, string message)` — pulls `amount` USDC from the caller via\n  `transferFrom` (caller must `approve` first), records `{sender, amount,\n  timestamp, message}` in the feed, and emits `NewTip`.\n- `getRecentTips(uint256 limit)` — the feed, **newest first**.\n- `tipsCount()`, `totalTipped()`, `tippedBy(address)`, `balance()` — read helpers\n  the UI uses for its stat tiles.\n- `withdraw(uint256)` / `transferOwnership(address)` — owner-only.\n\nThe USDC address is an immutable constructor argument, so the exact same contract\nworks against `MockUSDC` locally and real USDC on Base.\n\nRun the tests anytime:\n\n```bash\ncd contracts && forge test -vvv\n```\n\n---\n\n## Pointing at real Base\n\nThis exercise does **not** deploy anywhere. If you later wanted to run the UI\nagainst real Base (after deploying `TipJar` yourself with the real USDC address),\nset these in `frontend/.env.local` and restart `next dev`:\n\n```bash\nNEXT_PUBLIC_CHAIN_ID=8453\nNEXT_PUBLIC_RPC_URL=https://mainnet.base.org\nNEXT_PUBLIC_USDC_ADDRESS=0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\nNEXT_PUBLIC_TIPJAR_ADDRESS=<your deployed TipJar>\n```\n\nIn this mode the \"Get test USDC\" button disappears (real USDC has no `mint`), and\nyou'd fund your wallet with actual USDC. See `frontend/.env.local.example`.\n\n---\n\n## Troubleshooting\n\n- **\"No TipJar address configured\"** in the UI — you skipped the deploy, or\n  `deployment.local.json` wasn't synced. Re-run the deploy (step 2), then\n  `cd frontend && npm run sync-deployment` and restart the dev server.\n- **Feed is empty** — that's expected before the first tip. Send one.\n- **Wallet on the wrong network** — make sure MetaMask is on chain id `31337`.\n- **Restarted Anvil** — its state resets, so re-run the deploy script and\n  `npm run sync-deployment` (addresses will be the same on a fresh chain).\n```\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":158,"oldLines":4,"newStart":158,"newLines":3,"lines":[" - **Wallet on the wrong network** — make sure MetaMask is on chain id `31337`."," - **Restarted Anvil** — its state resets, so re-run the deploy script and","   `npm run sync-deployment` (addresses will be the same on a fresh chain).","-```"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD5c9HiRbtorjQ8ibzMk","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The USDC tip jar is built, running locally, and documented. Here's what I delivered.\n\n## What's here\n\nA two-part project in `workspace/`, verified end-to-end against a local Anvil chain:\n\n**Contract (`contracts/` — Foundry)**\n- `TipJar.sol` — takes the USDC address as an immutable constructor arg (real Base USDC `0x8335…` in production, a mock locally). `tip(amount, message)` pulls USDC via `transferFrom`, records `{sender, amount, timestamp, message}` in an onchain feed, and emits `NewTip`. Read helpers (`getRecentTips`, `tipsCount`, `totalTipped`, `balance`) back the UI; `withdraw`/`transferOwnership` are owner-only.\n- `MockUSDC.sol` — a 6-decimal ERC-20 stand-in so the whole thing runs offline with no real funds.\n- `DeployLocal.s.sol` — deploys both, mints test USDC to the Anvil accounts, and writes addresses to `frontend/deployment.local.json`.\n- **8 unit tests pass** (`forge test`) covering the feed, newest-first ordering, approval/zero-amount reverts, and owner-only withdraw.\n\n**Web app (`frontend/` — Next.js + wagmi + viem)**\n- **Connect-wallet flow** (`injected`/MetaMask, no WalletConnect project ID needed).\n- **Tip feed** + **stat tiles** (total tipped / count / jar balance), auto-refreshing.\n- **Send form** that handles the USDC `approve` → `tip` two-step, plus a local-only \"Get 1,000 test USDC\" mint button.\n- `sync-deployment.mjs` turns the deploy output into `.env.local` automatically.\n\n## Verified\n- `forge test` → 8/8 pass. Deploy script ran and wrote the addresses.\n- A real tip via `cast` (approve → tip) landed: `tipsCount=1`, feed returns `\"gm from cast!\"`, jar balance `5 USDC`.\n- `npm run build` compiles clean; dev server serves **HTTP 200** with all UI (Connect Wallet, Send a tip, Recent tips) rendering and no runtime errors.\n\nNothing was deployed to any live network. Setup is documented in `README.md` (prerequisites, the three-terminal quick start, wallet config, and how you'd point at real Base later).\n\nTwo notes worth flagging:\n- The machine already had an Anvil **forking Base** on port 8545 (leftover from another run). My deploy connected to it and worked fine — I left it alone rather than kill something I didn't start. The README's `anvil` step gives a fresh, reproducible chain for anyone else.\n- I had to work around a broken optional transitive dep in `wagmi/connectors` (Coinbase's `baseAccount` → `@x402/*`) by importing `injected` from `@wagmi/core` directly. Documented inline in `lib/wagmi.ts`.\n\nOne thing I couldn't do headlessly: click through the actual MetaMask browser flow. The contract path is proven via `cast` and the app serves and wires up to the RPC, but the in-browser wallet approval is the one step that needs a human with an extension installed."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":906,"cache_read_input_tokens":64425,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":906},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1c398c09-4d5b-4f28-a0e9-8dda39bfa970","uuid":"76a41c58-5d7d-42b5-adb8-15a12df91179","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:48:45.392Z","request_id":"req_011CeD5c8VqcGpRSMQ4mMtp3"}
